# Open items — standing ledger

The single list of everything still open in the project: owner questions, research, source fixes, sweeps and parked work. It replaces the "open/owner/TODO" tails scattered across workplans, handoffs and audit reports. Built 2026-10-03 from four scans (reports, old reports, ~/rebuild-state, content/docs), verified by grep and `git log` where cheap.
**How to use:** one line per item, with a stable ID (`A-07`) — refer to items by ID; new items take the next free number in their section, IDs are never reused; `- [ ]` open, `- [x]` done with date + commit. When the owner rules on an A item, record the ruling in the language TM / CLAUDE.md, tick the A item and add a D sweep below if text must change. Agents add new open items HERE, not in workplans. Prune `[x]` items older than ~2 weeks.
**Tags:** area `[cz] [uk] [en] [fr] [es] [all] [_original] [glossary] [frontend] [tooling] [db] [infra]`; effort `[S]` <1h · `[M]` one agent run · `[L]` multi-agent wave. `?` = status not confirmed cheaply. Source abbreviations: WP10 = WORKPLAN-2026-10-03-reading-conventions, WP09 = WORKPLAN-2026-09-25, HW = HANDOFF-rebuild-wave-2026-10-01, H28 = HANDOFF-rebuild-2026-09-28, UKF = UK-FABLELOUS-2026-09, RA = rebuild-audit-2026-09-29, OQ = ~/rebuild-state/OWNER-QUESTIONS-2026-10-03, TOQ = ~/rebuild-state/triage/OWNER-QUESTIONS, SI = ~/rebuild-state/source-issues.md.
**Phone view:** https://claude.ai/artifact/68AZXM55T21EwLs2E1BHvJ (private to the owner). Ticks and rulings typed there live in the artifact's db collection `marks` (doc id = item ID, fields `done`, `note`). To sync: `ArtifactData list marks` → apply to this file (tick, record rulings in TM, add D sweeps) → `uv run src/scripts/open_items_to_json.py <scratch>/open-items.json` → republish the page with that JSON → clear synced marks.

(3) ETIENNE.md (Georges's son, died young) was tagged on «le petit Etienne» = uncle Alexandre's son Etienne Alexandrovitch (index; «Alexandre et le petit Etienne son fils», 092/1881-06-02): removed in 067.0384/0386/0387; re-check the other ETIENNE tags (092/1881-06-02, 066/1876-09-15, 067/1876-10-14, …); the boy has no file; (4) KAPITAN.md vs KAPITANENKO.md: calibration says «Kapitan» → KAPITANENKO but 066/067 use KAPITAN throughout (10 vs 9 files repo-wide; (6) Mon Journal t.9 index errors: «Kissou» given as a nickname of Michel Eristoff and of Gritz — it is the cat (067.0540, 067.0549); Lola listed both as «Babanine (Marie Yégorovna)» and «Gorpintchenko (Marie), épouse Sperandio»; (7) TREGOUBOFF.md: 1876 «la fille du premier mariage de Domenica» (067.0632) vs 1881 «madame est la sœur de la petite tante, la femme à Etienne» (092/1881-06-11) — reconcile; (8) 067.0503 legacy RSR note (not bare) calls Pacha "likely a man she's involved with romantically" — he is her cousin Paul Gorpintchenko; (5) 067.0329 RSR note (parting was 20 May 1876, 060.0955); (6) Kissou is the cat («pendant que je caressais le chat», 067.0541), index slip; Lola is not an index slip (two Lolas, LOLA.md); (7) reconciled in TREGOUBOFF.md (Machenka née Kandiba is also a daughter of Domenica's first marriage); body rewritten), EXPOSITION (people/mentioned; calibration says EXPOSITION_UNIVERSELLE: 19 files still tag it, I retargeted 080/1878-05-11, 06-04, 06-05 only), LE_PAYS (newspaper under people/mentioned, 43 tags), JUVISY (a family under places/cities), CHAMBRES, and the 078/1878-03-03 clipping stubs CODE_PENAL, GAZETTE_DES_TRIBUNAUX, VAUCLUSE, ANNUAIRE_HISTORIQUE_DE_LESUR, CONSEIL_DE_GUERRE_DE_LA_SEINE (non-persons under people/); (4) now unreferenced: people/aristocracy/LE_VICOMTE_DE_BRAGELONE (duplicate of culture/literature/BRAGELONNE, tag removed at 080.0110) and people/mentioned/ROBERT (091.0470/0472 retagged JOHNSTONE) → retire; SUZANNE rewritten as Marie's mask name and its tag removed at 078.0494 (code name for Marie), but 078/1878-02-12 (078.0124) still tags it; (5) frontmatter entities list ALEXIS (Karageorgevitch) for the somnambulist in 078/1878-02-18, 02-19, 03-05 (tag at 078.0410 now ALEXIS_SOMNAMBULE) → entities-sync; (6) stale «CCC.DROPPED-NNNN» references in notes of 002/1873-02-25, 077/1878-01-17, 080/1878-06-20, 084/1879-02-23, 085/1879-06-21; (7) research: the «Grande Revue» of 20 June 1878; «la mélodie de Caen», «Si j'étais reine au beau pays de France» (080.0525); Patti's 1881 Paris house (091.0361 footnote says Théâtre-Italien, unverified); Larderel's marriage reported by Marcuard (080.0316), not in LARDEREL.md; the Cassagnac–«Thompson» duel of 2 March 1878 (probably Gaston Thomson, 078.0348); Mlle Blanc courted by Philippe de Bourbon (080.0141) — src: RSR nl2 2026-10-21 — **glossary run 2026-10-21:** done: (3) GRAND_16, EXPOSITION_UNIVERSELLE (+ body phase 3 uncommitted), CHAMBRES, CODE_PENAL, GAZETTE_DES_TRIBUNAUX, VAUCLUSE, ANNUAIRE_HISTORIQUE_DE_LESUR, CONSEIL_DE_GUERRE_DE_LA_SEINE moved (417a500064); (4) BRAGELONNE ← LE_VICOMTE_DE_BRAGELONE, ROBERT → JOHNSTONE (158ab526a0); open: EXPOSITION retags (queued), 078.0124 SUZANNE tag — **sweep 2026-10-22 (bsw-4, 067–078, uncommitted):** 067–078 part: (6) 077/1878-01-17 stale DROPPED reference annotated (merged into 077.0236 by the 10-01 rebuild); (7) the Cassagnac–Thomson duel of 2 March 1878 not found in a further search (078.0348 stays 0.6) — **sweep 2026-10-22 (000–027):** (6) 002/1873-02-25: the stale carnet 11 title-page reference explained in an RSR note at 002.0096 (the carnet 11 title page withdrawn 2026-09-29); other files outside range — **sweep 2026-10-22 (bsw-5):** (1) the tree footnotes were already patched; (2) 087.0884 «(!!!!)» and 087.0891 «sâle» fixed in _original after a page-image check (sync for the lead); (3) no EXPOSITION tags left in 079–106; (6) stale DROPPED refs corrected (080/1878-06-20, 084/1879-02-23; 085 already); (7) 080.0141 Mlle Blanc = probably Marie-Félix Blanc (0.7), MLLE_BLANC written; - [ ] B-63 [glossary][_original][cz][uk][en][S] **No-LAN entries g3 (096–105) open points (RSR, 2026-10-21)** — (1) footnote fixes made in _original only; patch by hand in the trees: 097/1882-11-18 [^2] (Sarah Bernhardt "whom the family knew socially" — unsupported; en, cz [^097.0106.1], uk [^097.0106.1]); 103/1884-01-12 [^5] ("architèque" = Emile Bastien-Lepage, not Gabriel Géry; "Adolphe" introduced by the song; Manet "recently pilloried" unsourced — en [^5], uk [^103.0004.1]; cz already right). (2) glossary moves/merges for the lead (not done): LIHOPOY is a person filed under places/hotels → people/mentioned; SAINT_AGATHE (people/religious) is the baron de Saint-Amand's nickname (t.15 index "Saint Amand (baron de) [Agathe, Putiphar]") → redirect to SAINT_AMAND; POPE and PERE_VASSILIEFF stubs are the same archpriest Wassilieff of 1882–84 (104.0513; t.16 index "Vassilieff (pope)") → merge; MME_DE_POURTALES / MADAME_DE_POURTALES / POURTALES triplicate stubs; JOUY / JOUY_EN_JOSAS duplicate stubs; NOVOIC_VREMIA, PARC_MONCEAU, L_EVENEMENT, LE_TEMPS filed as people/mentioned; THEATRE_DES_ITALIENS / people/mentioned/ITALIENS duplicate. (3) research: no works entry for Tony Robert-Fleury's 1882 portrait of Marie (096.0003, 096.0033, 096.0332, 097.0101–0105; price 4,000 fr); 101.0464 "la dame aux jambes maigres" source unfound; 105.0229 artist's funeral in the rue Ampère (17 July 1884) unidentified; 105.0184 the 1884 Maupassant volume with an etched portrait unidentified; 104.0215 which Faure. (4) new glossary entries: GARNITSKY, HELENE_BERTAUX, PRINCESSE_MASSALSKY, CHARLES_HAYEM, CLOVIS_HUGUES; ARIANE_STATUE extended (1879 sketch, 096.0160), GABRIEL_GERY (father = Charles Géry per index); run entities-sync for 096–105. — src: nolan-entries brief, RSR g3 — **glossary run 2026-10-21:** done: (2) LIHOPOY, NOVOIC_VREMIA, PARC_MONCEAU, L_EVENEMENT, LE_TEMPS moved (417a500064); SAINT_AMAND ← SAINT_AGATHE (alias added), MME_DE_POURTALES ← MADAME_DE_POURTALES, POURTALES, JOUY_EN_JOSAS ← JOUY then moved, THEATRE_DES_ITALIENS ← ITALIENS (3 «Italiens» = people tags removed) (158ab526a0); (3) PORTRAIT_DE_MARIE_ROBERT_FLEURY created, tags queued (phase 3 uncommitted); open: POPE/PERE_VASSILIEFF — **research + apply 2026-10-22 (uncommitted):** (2) POPE = Pius IX (009–067); 1878–84 «le pope» → PERE_VASSILIEFF (17 retags incl. 081.0369, 20 untagged ¶ added; not the Gavrontsi priest 096.0464/0471); no merge — **sweep 2026-10-22 (bsw-5):** (1) the tree footnotes were already patched; (3) 105.0229 = probably the painter-engraver Alphonse Hirsch, d. 15 July 1884, of 63 rue Ampère (AGORHA/INHA) 0.8; 104.0215 Faure = probably Maurice Faure the félibre (0.75); 105.0184 candidates *Miss Harriet* / *Les Sœurs Rondoli* (open); 101.0464 open

(4) missing files (no tag added): places — the Soden Kurhaus, Konotop, Chislehurst, rue de Rivoli (097.0362, 085.0054; only people/mentioned stubs), église Saint-Augustin (085.0453, 0490; SAINT_AUGUSTIN is the saint), Musée du Luxembourg (089.0006); culture — the newspapers Le Voltaire (094.0465–0466; VOLTAIRE is the writer) and L'Appel au peuple (083.0341), the ordre du Lion et du Soleil (080.0710); people — the marquise de Galliffet (086.0125; GALLIFFET is the general), the comte de Girardin (088.0004, «rien d'Emile»), Delphine de Girardin (089.0395), Mme de Rothschild (087.0291), Marie d'Audiffret and her sisters (088.0251 etc.; AUDIFFRET_MME is the mother), Cherubini the skater (086.0752), the Variétés actor Baron (084.0146; BARON stub empty), Marie the 1881 house servant (091.0324). (5) unsure, left untagged: 089.0444 «Olga est mariée… Marie depuis cinq mois» (which Olga and Marie), 089.0602 «les filles de Wladimir», 085.0527 the joke invitation (are «le père Léon», «le fils Arnaud» Gambetta and Arnaud de l'Ariège?), 077.0339 «Nini» in a masked 1878 letter (not Paul's wife). — src: tagcand-hi review — **glossary run 2026-10-22:** done (phase 4 uncommitted): (2) merged MLLE_SCHAEPPI, OPERA_DE_PARIS/OPERA_PARIS → OPERA, M_DE_VALLON, COLONEL_TALLANDIER, MARQUIS_DE_BILIONI/M_BILLIOTI, PHILIPPINI/M_PHILIPPINI, MADRAZZO, WODZINSKY (B-69), GRATTAMINARDA, BOULDOKOFF, SAN_CESARIO, ALPHONSE, DA_COSTA, JOSEPH_ARNAUD, MLLE_ACARD/MME_DE_CASSAGNAC → JULIA_ACARD, JULIETTE_LAMBER → MME_ADAM, DR_FAUVEL → FAUVEL (MISS_HALL, MADEMOISELLE_OELSNITZ, PERONNEY, DUGUE done 158ab526a0); (5) 077.0339 «Nini» still open — **sweep 2026-10-22 (bsw-5, 079–106 part):** (3) Florence: FLORENCE_LACON = Florence herself, FOSTER = the Foster family/Arthur (bodies written; FLORENCE_FOSTER should redirect to FLORENCE_LACON; 023–077 FOSTER tags meaning Florence to check, outside range); 089.0037 noted (Julie de Rothschild, tag kept on ROTHSCHILD_ADOLPHE); 091.0102 «Nadine» = the aunt (t.14 index), NADINE tag removed; (4) new MUSEE_DU_LUXEMBOURG (079.0238, 089.0006, 100.0270), LE_VOLTAIRE (11 ¶ in 087–104), MARIE_D_AUDIFFRET + AUDIFFRET_MME body (1880 «les Audiffret» retagged from EMILE_D_AUDIFFRET), MARQUISE_DE_GALLIFFET; the saint tag SAINT_AUGUSTIN removed where the church is meant (079.0007, 079.0339, 081.0098, 085.0453, 085.0490); open: (1) 004–064, place stubs under people/ (moves), Delphine/comte de Girardin, Mme de Rothschild 087.0291, Cherubini, Baron, Marie the servant (single mentions) — **apply 2026-10-22 (lead apply, uncommitted):** (3) FOSTER → FLORENCE_LACON on 46 paragraphs where Florence herself is meant (023–028: her name, «Mlle/la petite Foster», «elle»; - [ ] B-66 [glossary][_original][S] **Tag-scout review 049–061 open points (tag-scout rows, 2026-10-21)** — (1) unsure rows left untagged: 054.0415, 054.0418, 055.0694 «Bergerault» in Rome (incl. a dreamed «violoniste del Re») — same man as BELLE_DE_JOUR (George Bergerault of Nice)?; 055.0922, 056.0153, 056.0157, 056.0166 «les Antonelli» as a family — no family file (ANTONELLI = Pietro); 049.0415, 049.0416, 049.0419 «Jeanne» in Nice, Nov 1875 — identity unknown (JEANNE_DANILOFF?; JEANNE.md is an 1879 stub); 050.0739, 050.0811 «Mme de Lewin» — probably Clémentine Durand (married John Lewin, 019.0104), no own file (LEWIN.md = the man); 049.0187 «Stabat Mater» sung by the Théâtre Italien chorus — which setting (STABAT_MATER_PERGOLESI?); 053.0259 «une des Anglaises est mariée à un Johnstone» — which Johnstone; 053.0332 «Frédéric» in Rome (a nickname) — referent unknown. (2) VISCONTI.md describes a Nice ticket business (1873) but its tags in 053–056 are the Roman baron Visconti: split or rewrite. (3) PANTHEON.md (places/landmarks) is an empty auto-stub saying «Type: people - mentioned»; its uses (052, 060, 077) are the Rome Pantheon — research. (4) LYON.md (places/cities) body calls itself a «literary reference»; it is the city. (5) the princess Galitzine who died in Dec 1875 («la petite blonde Nathalie Galitzine», 050.0824; 052.0089) is not BETE (Bête still alive 1876): no file, MISSING. (6) footnote 059.0778 says «The Apollo Theatre in Nice»; it is the Teatro Apollo in Rome (054.0448) — source fix, then sync. (7) duplicate stubs: SAINTE_MARIE / VIRGIN_MARY (Virgin Mary), NAPOLEON_I / NAPOLEON_IER, AMERIQUE (people stub, 0 uses) / places AMERICA, ISSAYEVITCH / PAUL_YSSAYEVITCH, CLEMENT / TORLONIA, FORTUNE (1873–75) vs CHOCOLAT (1876).
